cargo each day. Goods from the ships are usually
distributed throughout the region by train or by
truck. However, sometimes the goods need to wait
for days before they are transported, ®' which can
cause problems due to lack of affordable storage
space. Unfortunately, lease costs for storage space
in the area are too high for many shipping companies
to afford. After the break, we’ll hear from ®? Pablo
Alvarez, owner of Alva Shipping, about this issue.
So stay tuned!

business correspondence. Business correspondence
has different guidelines than the informal type of
writing you use when writing to a friend. For example,
when writing to a friend you might address them
by their first name or perhaps include emojis. You
know—symbols like smiley faces that are commonly
used to express feelings. However, ®’emojis are
considered inappropriate in business writing.

include the recipient’s title and last name.
Remember, we don’t want to offend our clients.
Now, | will project some slides of informally worded
e-mails. In your notebooks, “rewrite the e-mails
using wording and style appropriate for business
correspondence.
